## 57. hw279-hw280 - a hive industry produces at its size, random seeds from day 0 (2026-10-26)

- **hw279-hw280** (2026-10-26 14:15, the user: "i would prefer to have no planet or size not dependent on fuel or metals. is it possible to just have the mine give the same as the size? rather than size - 2?" -> "go"; jar 14:15:05 = `threatinc_hiveOutputOffset` 2 on a hive Mining's and Fuel Production's supply bonus and Refining's demand reduction (`ThreatColonyManager.applyHiveOutput`, re-asserted by `FabricationCore.apply`), on top of the four leftover fixes; driver `scratchpad/seeds-hw174/run-seeds-12.ps1`, hw279 set B (a OceanPena, b ZhiFarah, c AphelionDysnomia), hw280 set A, from day 0 `-Days 3800 -Snapshots 300`, watcher `watch-seeds-12.sh`). Read against hw278 (every hive held at size 3 or 2) and the day-0 tables (s48, hw239). What to see: hives climbing past 3 where a mine and a plant stand, phase 2 (scouts, places known) and an opening strike, and whether a cluster on moderate deposits reaches 8.
  **hw279 read at w1,485-1,604 (14:30): THE LIFT WORKS WHERE A MINE STANDS, AND TWO MORE LEFTOVERS SHOW.** a OceanPena 30 waves -> 34 hives, scouts 16; c AphelionDysnomia 74 waves -> 73 hives, scouts 21, fuel made 3,000/mo, fleets 426 FP; b ZhiFarah 4 hives at size 2 (no volatiles in its cluster, fuel made 0). But every hive is size 2 ("size 68" over 34, "147" over 73), "held at the next size's floor" 34 / 4 / 61, phase 1 everywhere, 0 strikes. (1) Every wave desizes its source - "Budded from Strathcona: size 4 -> 3" before each "Seeding Swarm from Strathcona": `chargeFP` keeps only `shiftFloorStrata` (800) while the gate (`poolableFP` -> `spareFP`) keeps `sizeAt(size)`, so the seed's 400 strata came out from under the size; the homes churn 3 <-> 4 and seed again. (2) A size-2 seed never grows to 3: `nextSizeFed` read fuel 0 of 1 - vanilla computes no availability for a commodity the market does not demand, and a size-2 Core demands no fuel - so metals 1 short + fuel 1 "short" = HOLD at 2 on every seed, mine or no mine. **Fix 5 (built after hw279 ends):** `chargeFP(market, fp, keepSize)` with the size's floor for a wave's two charges; `nextSizeFed` ignores an input the world does not demand yet (its first unit is asked at the new size, where one short is a HOLD); the "Hive worlds" census names three held worlds' inputs (`inputsText`). Driver stopped before set A (14:35); round 54 = hw280 (set B) + hw281 (set A).
  **hw279 end (14:50, w3,819-3,864, 0 exceptions):** a OceanPena 34 hives (30 waves, 16 scouts), b ZhiFarah 4, c AphelionDysnomia 173 hives (171 waves, 21 scouts) - every one size 2 ("size 347" over 173, "held at the next size's floor 167"), phase 1 everywhere, 0 strikes; the spread without growth. Fix 5 jar 14:51:22.

## 79. hw304 - round 72, CONSUMPTION from the hw302 snapshots (2026-10-28 09:30-)

The user's plan after section 78 (facts DECISION 09:30): the swarm CONSUMES each claimed industry over
its build time and removes it, military last, no lump reward, as many at once as districts held; a
world with nothing left to claim is left (the pool and troops re-embark for the nearest hive) and the
owner's builder restores what was eaten, quickest first. Built 09:28 (jar 09:28:55, 0e343617;
`occupation.md` 7). hw304a = hw302a d1,293 (OceanPena), hw304b = hw302b d1,263 (ZhiFarah), 1,500 days,
two games, driver run-seeds-29.ps1. Read: industries consumed and the clocks ("has consumed"), worlds
gutted and left ("lifts off"), the builder's restores, districts taken / retaken, exceptions.

**hw304 VOID (09:35, stopped at war day ~1,450):** every occupation lifted off within 60-120 days with "0 industries consumed, nothing left to claim" (Athulf, Tigra City, Derinkuyu, Orthrus) or after the military alone (Yama: Ground Defenses, then off - its Farming, Light Industry, Refining and Heavy Industry claims of hw302b dropped at load). Cause: the plugins' `isBuilding` true made vanilla read a consumed producer as not functional and clear its supply, `producers` filtered it out as "no supply", the tick dropped the claim as no longer claimable, and `next` found only the military. What worked: the clocks and removals (Ground Defenses at 60 d, Patrol HQ 60, Heavy Batteries 90), the lift-off fleets (564-1,508 FP bound for the nearest hive), the restores (Ilm, Raesvelg, Yama) and the holds. **Fix 1 (jar 09:37:17):** the Occupied plugins override `isFunctional` (disrupted / vanilla-building / upgrading only), and `producers` keeps a claimed producer whatever its live supply reads. hw305 = round 73, the same snapshots.

## 83. hw308 - round 76, day-0 seeds on the consumption jar; THE HANG FOUND (2026-10-28 11:23-12:26)

Set B from day 0 on jar 10:10:43 (fixes 1-3), 3,800 days, 75 minutes, driver run-seeds-33.ps1. Stopped at
12:26 (a and c at war ~3,100-3,200, a mid-run read) to build fix 4; hw309 never ran.

| game | war day | hives | consumed | lift-offs | restores | claims / reclaims / rebuilds | occupied / retaken | exc |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| hw308a | 3,197 | 170 | 93 | 31 | 42 | 125 / 16 / 7 | 13 / 16 | 0 |
| hw308b | 1,296 (HUNG 11:43) | 93 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 2 / 0 / 0 | 0 / 0 | 0 |
| hw308c | 3,061 | 260 | 156 | 54 | 37 | 217 / 34 / 14 | 40 / 34 | 0 |

- **The hang is ours, and found.** hw308b froze at campaign day 1,272 (war 1,296) right after a
  snapshot save, as hw306a had (campaign day 325 / war 1,622). A `jstack` of the three games (the
  bundled jre is OpenJDK 17, so JDK 17's jstack attaches) showed a and c in vanilla's fleet AI and b's
  campaign thread RUNNABLE in both dumps with 1,020 threatinc frames repeating:
  `ThreatOccupation.producers` -> `OccupiedLightIndustry.isBuilding` -> `ThreatOccupation.consuming`
  -> `active` -> `producers` ... Fix 3a had made `producers` ask `ind.isBuilding()` of an unclaimed
  industry with no supply; the Occupied plugins' override answers through `consuming`, which lists
  the active claims, which lists the producers. The `StackOverflowError` was caught by `consuming`'s
  `catch (Throwable)`, so no exception was logged and the tick began again from the next industry -
  a loop with no end, the clock standing. It fires only when an unclaimed producer has an empty
  supply read at the tick (a Military Base or Ground Defenses out of the producers list, a disrupted
  industry, a fresh build), which is why hw307a's re-run did not reproduce it.
- **Fix 4 (12:26, jar 12:26:44, a1be8feb):** `producers` and `release` read vanilla's own building
  flag through `OccupiedIndustry.underConstruction()` (each Occupied plugin returns
  `super.isBuilding()`), never the override; `consuming` rethrows a `StackOverflowError` instead of
  swallowing it. **hw310 / hw311 (rounds 78-79)** re-run the day-0 seeds on it from 12:27.
- a and c, to the stop: the cycle as in hw305-hw307 (93 / 156 consumed, 31 / 54 lift-offs, 42 / 37
  restores, 16 / 34 districts retaken), hives 170 / 260 at d3,100-3,200 - set B's spread as before.

## 87. hw314-hw315 - rounds 82-83, day-0 seeds on the merged jar 22:29:54 (2026-10-28 22:31-)

The general seed test after the pull (the user: "pull and build jar then run general seed test
monitoring the recent changes in particular"). Local main fast-forwarded 2b332641 -> fece9daa (fourteen
commits from the other machine), jar rebuilt here 22:29:54. Driver `run-seeds-36.ps1`, hw314 set B
(OceanPena / ZhiFarah / AphelionDysnomia), hw315 set A, from day 0 `-Days 3800 -Snapshots 300`, 75
minutes a round; watcher `watch-seeds-36.sh` + `check-grand.sh` (`grandidle` on the ignore list);
tasks ThreatIncSeeds36 / WatchSeeds36 (`/sd 01.01.2030`, `/run`).

What the jar carries beyond hw312 / hw313's lump jar, all "untested" on commit - the recent changes
to read for:
- the share rule (`ThreatGroundFronts.lineRateOf`: a pusher gains covered force x its share of the
  field, the other's push subtracted), pressure breaks cover (`coverPressure`), the bleed-out doctrine
  (`pushPaysOff` - NPC and swarm push while the next district comes before the force bleeds out);
- manned defences (`mannedGarrison`: ground defence counts as far as armed marines man it, 20 points
  a marine; `ThreatReserves.floor` keeps the manning need at home) - NPC colonies short of marines
  now fall faster; the effect scales with what stands of a consumed / rebuilding structure;
- consumption fixes 5 and 6 (military claims consume in free slots; `releaseAll` on the last district
  retaken), the occupation's army rising against the force over it (spare troops off the front);
- a hostile station over the swarm's front makes the next strike an expedition (`stationOverFront`).

Read against hw312 (set B) / hw313 (set A): hives 274-350, consumed 62-217, lift-offs 13-82, restores
22-90, retaken 43-71, 0 exceptions. What to see: districts taken and retaken at a different pace (the
share rule and manned defences both move the line), "Front collapsed" / "Threat ground victory" counts,
expeditions over stations, the hive count unchanged, 0 exceptions on the new paths.
